## 2026-03-31 Procedural Fill Progress
- Priority remains:
- biome -> world fill -> real underwater world

### Done for real
- Stage 1 procedural fill moved from "smart descriptions" to visible proxy world content.
- The scatter system is now layered:
- `Ground`
- `Cluster`
- `Structure`
- `Spawn`
- The Stage 1 catalog now has working family/rule coverage for the current procedural set.
- Proxy family generation now creates multiple variants per family.
- Preview fallback is now explicit and inspectable instead of hidden.

### Verified in Unity
- no errors/warnings in console after:
- `Build Procedural Fill Foundations`
- `Rebuild World Runtime Stack`
- `Rebuild Procedural Scatter Preview`
- `Validate MapMagic World Stack`
- `__PROCEDURAL_SCATTER_WORLD` exists in the scene
- active field-driven scatter instances: `201`
- total `WorldProceduralProxyInstance` in scene: `358`
- layer counts:
- `Ground = 139`
- `Cluster = 21`
- `Structure = 6`
- `Spawn = 35`

### Honest reading
- the world is still proxy-based, not final art
- but it is no longer empty or purely declarative
- it now visibly has:
- floor nature
- pockets
- ruins / structures
- spawn anchors
- next value is not more abstraction, but stronger biome-specific distribution and better use of real MapMagic fields when available

## 2026-03-31 Procedural Fill Progress B

### Done for real
- Stage 1 procedural rules now actually use biome-family and zone-kind filters.
- This was not just code-level intention:
- `Build Procedural Fill Foundations` rewrote rule assets with real preferred biome families and preferred zone kinds.
- `WorldProceduralFieldSampler` now has synthetic fallback biome assignment when live biome data is missing.
- The same sampler now derives a synthetic zone hint for preview when no real zone anchor is active.

### Why this matters
- Before this, a lot of Stage 1 rules were still effectively too generic in preview.
- Or worse: they had biome/zone intent in code, but no real biome/zone source to drive them.
- Now preview can still make a believable choice even when:
- MapMagic is not available
- no zone is currently active
- live biome family is missing

### Verified in Unity
- console: `0 errors / 0 warnings`
- scene saved: `Assets/_Project/Scenes/02_HECTON_WORLD.unity`
- sampler fallback families are assigned
- sampler fallback biome is no longer `None`
- current debug biome: `Sediment Drift`
- current active field-driven scatter instances: `138`
- current scene total `WorldProceduralProxyInstance`: `295`
- current layer counts:
- `Ground = 68`
- `Cluster = 21`
- `Structure = 46`
- `Spawn = 3`

### Honest reading
- the preview is now more selective
- less "everything everywhere"
- more "this area behaves like a certain underwater character"
- real MapMagic biome fields are still the next stronger step
- but the preview is no longer blocked waiting for them

## 2026-03-31 Procedural Fill Progress C

### Done for real
- `WorldPrefabFamilyProfile` now has soft biome/zone affinity.
- I did not duplicate those lists manually into family authoring.
- Instead, family affinity is now derived automatically from Stage 1 procedural rules during `Build Procedural Fill Foundations`.
- `WorldProceduralScatterDirector` now adds family affinity into the placement score.
- I also fixed the diagnostics path where `Structure` and `Spawn` could be rescue-placed but still show top family as `None`.

### Why this matters
- Before this pass, the preview could know "this rule is allowed here" but still feel too generic.
- Now the family itself has a preference for its kind of water.
- That makes the proxy world feel more like:
- shelter/resource water
- reef/navigation water
- service/power/rift water
- instead of one generic valid soup.

### Verified in Unity
- console: `0 errors / 0 warnings`
- scene saved: `Assets/_Project/Scenes/02_HECTON_WORLD.unity`
- total `WorldProceduralProxyInstance`: `385`
- active scatter children under `__PROCEDURAL_SCATTER_WORLD`: `228`

- current sampler zone: `Synthetic:Resources`

- `Ground = 73`
- `Cluster = 97`
- `Structure = 47`
- `Spawn = 11`

- `Ground = Kelp Tall`
- `Cluster = Pocket Safe`

- `Spawn = Creature Spawn Passive`

### Honest reading
- this is a real product step, not a cosmetic refactor
- the proxy world now has stronger underwater character even without live MapMagic biome delivery
- next strong step is not more authoring abstractions
- next strong step is pushing this same system toward real biome-specific patterns and better balance between shelter/resource/hazard/service water
